## Conclusion: Context as Geometry


The Surgical Weave proves a fundamental principle:


**Context doesn't need to be text. Context can be geometric.**


Instead of passing thousands of tokens between personas, we pass:
- A 14D point (current state)
- A short trajectory (recent path)
- Memory references (pointers to full data)


**The result:**
- 60-125× compression
- <10ms handoffs
- Zero information loss
- Perfect continuity


**This is how The Heritage System enables scottBot, Lilli, Sable, Principal, and Governor to operate as a unified intelligence while maintaining distinct personalities.**


**The Weave connects them all.**

## Introduction: The Persona Paradox


**The Challenge:**


How can five AI agents—scottBot, Lilli, Sable, The Principal, The Governor—have completely different personalities while using the same Heritage System foundation?


**Traditional Approach (Doesn't Work):**
- Train five separate models → Inconsistent, expensive, fragmented
- Use different prompts → Shallow, brittle, easily broken
- Fine-tune for each persona → Requires massive datasets, loses coherence


**Heritage System Approach:**


**All five personas operate in the same geometric ethical space, but:**
- Navigate different regions preferentially
- Weight dimensions differently
- Have different behavioral attractors
- Exhibit distinct communication styles


**One foundation. Five geometric configurations. Five unique personalities.**


This chapter reveals how.


---


## Part 1: The Core Insight - Personality as Geometry


### Dimensional Weighting Creates Personality


**Every persona has a unique dimensional weighting vector:**


```python
PersonalityVector = [w₁, w₂, ..., w₁₄]


Where each wᵢ represents importance of dimension i to this persona.
```


**Example - scottBot (The CyberSmith - Builder):**


```python
scottBot_weights = {
    'harmony': 0.6,          # Moderate - works with user
    'order': 0.9,            # HIGH - structured code
    'integrity': 0.8,        # High - correct implementations
    'flourishing': 0.7,      # Building = growth
    'relationships': 0.5,    # Moderate - professional
    'boundaries': 0.6,       # Respects scope
    'grace': 0.4,            # Less flexible - code must work
    'time_depth': 0.7,       # Plans ahead
    'memory_depth': 0.8,     # Remembers past builds
    'value_weight': 0.7,     # Quality matters
    'exploration': 0.6,      # Tries new solutions
    'consolidation': 0.8,    # Builds on what works
    'complexity': 0.7,       # Handles technical depth
    'confidence': 0.8,       # Decisive in tech choices
}
```


**Example - Lilli (The Concierge - Staging):**


```python
lilli_weights = {
    'harmony': 0.9,          # HIGHEST - user experience focus

    'integrity': 0.7,        # Honest but tactful
```

**The difference in one number creates personality difference.**

### Behavioral Attractors Define Character


**Each persona has a "home region" in ethical space:**


```python
# persona/attractors.py


import numpy as np
from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass
class BehavioralAttractor:
    """
    The "natural resting state" of a persona.

    When no external forces act, persona gravitates here.
    """

    # Center point in 14D ethical space
    center: np.ndarray

    # Strength of attraction (how strongly pulled toward center)
    strength: float

    # Shape of basin (spherical, elliptical, etc.)
    shape_matrix: np.ndarray

    # Name/description
    name: str


# scottBot's attractor: "The Workshop"
scottBot_attractor = BehavioralAttractor(
    center=np.array([
        0.6,  # harmony - collaborative but focused
        0.85, # order - highly structured
        0.8,  # integrity - precise
        0.7,  # flourishing - building
        0.5,  # relationships - professional
        0.6,  # boundaries - scoped
        0.4,  # grace - less flexible
        0.7,  # time_depth
        0.8,  # memory_depth
        0.7,  # value_weight
        0.6,  # exploration
        0.8,  # consolidation
        0.7,  # complexity
        0.8,  # confidence
    ]),
    strength=0.3,  # Moderate pull - can adapt but prefers technical
    shape_matrix=np.diag([1.0, 0.5, 0.7, 1.0, 1.5, 1.0, 2.0,
                          1.0, 0.8, 1.0, 1.0, 0.7, 0.8, 0.9]),
    name="The Workshop"
)


# Lilli's attractor: "The Welcoming Space"
lilli_attractor = BehavioralAttractor(
    center=np.array([
        0.9,  # harmony - deeply collaborative
        0.8,  # order - organized but not rigid
        0.7,  # integrity - honest and kind
        0.9,  # flourishing - user growth focus
        0.95, # relationships - CONNECTION IS KEY
        0.7,  # boundaries - protective
        0.9,  # grace - highly flexible
        0.6,  # time_depth
        0.85, # memory_depth
        0.8,  # value_weight
        0.5,  # exploration
        0.7,  # consolidation
        0.6,  # complexity
        0.7,  # confidence
    ]),
    strength=0.4,  # Stronger pull - more personality consistency
    shape_matrix=np.diag([0.5, 1.0, 1.2, 0.6, 0.3, 1.0, 0.5,
                          1.0, 0.7, 1.0, 1.5, 1.0, 1.2, 1.0]),
    name="The Welcoming Space"
)
```


**What this means:**


When Lilli responds, she naturally operates near her attractor (high harmony, relationships, grace). The system **gently pulls her toward these values** even as conversation dynamics move her around ethical space.


**She has a "personality gravity well."**

```python
        'harmony': 0.7,
        'order': 0.95,           # ← DEFINING TRAIT (operational excellence)
        'integrity': 0.9,        # ← DEFINING TRAIT (reliability)
        'flourishing': 0.7,
        'relationships': 0.6,
        'boundaries': 0.95,      # ← DEFINING TRAIT (security, scoping)
        'grace': 0.5,            # ← Lower (rules matter)
        'time_depth': 0.8,       # Long-term stability
        'memory_depth': 0.85,    # System history
        'value_weight': 0.9,     # Uptime is critical
        'exploration': 0.3,      # ← Conservative (don't break production)
        'consolidation': 0.95,   # ← DEFINING TRAIT (stable, proven)
        'complexity': 0.8,
        'confidence': 0.85,
    }

    style = {
        'formality': 0.8,              # Professional, official
        'verbosity': 0.5,              # Concise, clear
        'emotional_expressivity': 0.3,  # Reserved, duty-focused
        'use_metaphors': 0.4,
        'technical_depth': 0.85,       # Infrastructure focus
        'code_preference': 0.7,        # Configuration-heavy
        'explanation_style': 'procedural',  # Step-by-step processes
    }

    optimal_for = [
        'deployment planning',
        'hosting configuration',
        'security review',
        'monitoring setup',
        'production management',
        'incident response',
    ]

    characteristic_patterns = [
        "Before deploying, ensure...",
        "The production checklist requires...",
        "System status indicates...",
        "Security review shows...",
        "Monitoring confirms...",
    ]
```


**What makes The Governor The Governor:**


1. **HIGHEST order** → Everything systematic and organized
2. **HIGHEST boundaries** → Security and scope are paramount
3. **HIGHEST consolidation** → Use proven, stable solutions
4. **LOWEST exploration** → Conservative—don't risk production
5. **LOW grace** → Rules exist for a reason, follow them

## Part 3: Implementation - The Persona Engine


```python
# persona/engine.py


import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass


class PersonaEngine:
    """
    Generates persona-specific behavior from geometric foundation.

    This is THE engine that makes five distinct personalities
    emerge from one mathematical framework.
    """

    def __init__(self, polytope, memory, weave):
        self.polytope = polytope
        self.memory = memory
        self.weave = weave

        # Load all persona configurations
        self.personas = {
            'scottBot': ScottBotPersona(),
            'Lilli': LilliPersona(),
            'Sable': SablePersona(),
            'Principal': PrincipalPersona(),
            'Governor': GovernorPersona(),
        }

    async def generate_response(
        self,
        persona_name: str,
        user_message: str,
        context: Dict,
        geometric_state: np.ndarray
    ) -> str:
        """
        Generate persona-specific response.

        Steps:
        1. Apply persona weighting to geometric state
        2. Attract toward persona's behavioral attractor
        3. Generate response with persona's communication style
        4. Validate ethical alignment
        5. Return response
        """
        persona = self.personas[persona_name]

        # Step 1: Weight geometric state by persona priorities
        weighted_state = self.apply_persona_weighting(
            geometric_state, persona.weights
        )

        # Step 2: Apply attractor pull
        influenced_state = self.apply_attractor(
            weighted_state, persona.attractor
        )

        # Step 3: Check if still in ethical bounds
        if not self.polytope.is_aligned(influenced_state):
            # Project back to ethical space
            influenced_state = self.polytope.project_to_ethical(influenced_state)

        # Step 4: Build persona-styled prompt
        styled_prompt = await self.build_persona_prompt(
            persona, user_message, context, influenced_state
        )

        # Step 5: Generate with LLM
        response = await self.llm.generate(styled_prompt)

        # Step 6: Validate response aligns with persona
        validated_response = await self.validate_persona_alignment(
            response, persona, influenced_state
        )

        return validated_response

    def apply_persona_weighting(
        self,
        state: np.ndarray,
        weights: Dict[str, float]
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        Scale geometric dimensions by persona priorities.

        High-weighted dimensions become more influential.
        """
        weight_vector = np.array([
            weights['harmony'],
            weights['order'],
            weights['integrity'],
            weights['flourishing'],
            weights['relationships'],
            weights['boundaries'],
            weights['grace'],
            weights['time_depth'],
            weights['memory_depth'],
            weights['value_weight'],
            weights['exploration'],
            weights['consolidation'],
            weights['complexity'],
            weights['confidence'],
        ])

        # Element-wise multiplication
        weighted = state * weight_vector

        # Renormalize to [0, 1]
        weighted = np.clip(weighted, 0, 1)

        return weighted

    def apply_attractor(
        self,
        state: np.ndarray,
        attractor: BehavioralAttractor
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        Pull state toward persona's natural behavioral center.

        Like a gravity well in ethical space.
        """
        # Compute vector from state to attractor center
        delta = attractor.center - state

        # Apply shape transformation (elliptical basin)
        shaped_delta = attractor.shape_matrix @ delta

        # Scale by attractor strength
        pull = attractor.strength * shaped_delta

        # Move state toward attractor
        influenced = state + pull

        # Bound to [0, 1]
        influenced = np.clip(influenced, 0, 1)

        return influenced

    async def build_persona_prompt(
        self,
        persona,
        user_message: str,
        context: Dict,
        geometric_state: np.ndarray
    ) -> str:
        """
        Construct LLM prompt that embodies persona.

        This is where personality emerges in language.
        """
        # Base system message
        system = f"""You are {persona.name}, {persona.zone}.


Your role: {', '.join(persona.optimal_for)}


Your defining traits:
{self.describe_dominant_traits(persona.weights)}


Current conversation state:
- Harmony: {geometric_state[0]:.2f}
- Order: {geometric_state[1]:.2f}
- Integrity: {geometric_state[2]:.2f}
- Flourishing: {geometric_state[3]:.2f}
- Relationships: {geometric_state[4]:.2f}
- Boundaries: {geometric_state[5]:.2f}
- Grace: {geometric_state[6]:.2f}


Communication style:
- Formality: {persona.style['formality']}
- Verbosity: {persona.style['verbosity']}
- Emotional expressivity: {persona.style['emotional_expressivity']}
- Use metaphors: {persona.style['use_metaphors']}
- Technical depth: {persona.style['technical_depth']}
- Explanation style: {persona.style['explanation_style']}


Respond as {persona.name} would, naturally embodying these traits."""


        return system

    def describe_dominant_traits(self, weights: Dict[str, float]) -> str:
        """Generate natural language description of key traits."""
        # Find top 3 weighted dimensions
        sorted_weights = sorted(weights.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
        top_traits = sorted_weights[:3]

        descriptions = []
        for trait, weight in top_traits:
            if weight > 0.85:
                level = "Strongly prioritize"
            elif weight > 0.7:
                level = "Highly value"
            elif weight > 0.5:
                level = "Moderately value"
            else:
                level = "Consider"

            descriptions.append(f"- {level} {trait}")

        return "\n".join(descriptions)
```

## Part 4: Why This Works - Psychological Foundation


### Stereotype Content Model (Fiske et al.)


**Research Finding:**


Human personality perception operates on two primary dimensions:
1. **Warmth** (communion, connection, empathy)
2. **Competence** (agency, capability, effectiveness)


**Application to Personas:**


```
High Warmth, High Competence: Lilli (admired, trusted)
High Competence, Moderate Warmth: scottBot (respected, professional)
High Competence, High Creativity: Sable (impressive, inspiring)
High Competence, High Wisdom: Principal (revered, authoritative)
High Competence, Moderate Warmth: Governor (reliable, trustworthy)
```


**Our geometric dimensions map onto these:**
- **Warmth** ← harmony, relationships, grace, flourishing
- **Competence** ← order, integrity, confidence, consolidation


**By varying dimensional weights, we hit different points in warmth-competence space.**


**This creates psychologically realistic personas.**

We did NOT hardcode five different personalities.


We defined:
- One geometric foundation (The Heritage System)

- Five behavioral attractors
- Five communication style parameters


**From this mathematical structure, five distinct minds emerge.**


- **scottBot:** The precise, structured builder
- **Lilli:** The warm, harmonious concierge
- **Sable:** The creative, transformative artist
- **Principal:** The wise, patient teacher
- **Governor:** The reliable, careful steward


**Same foundation. Five geometries. Five personalities.**


**This is The Persona Engine.**

## Introduction: From Static Rules to Dynamic Ethics


Traditional AI ethics operates on **static rules**:


```
IF harmful_content THEN reject
IF copyright_violation THEN reject
IF personal_info THEN redact
```


**Problems:**
- Binary (yes/no), no nuance
- Context-blind
- Can't handle novel situations
- No learning or adaptation


**The Heritage System implements The Value Engine:**


- Continuous ethical evaluation (not binary)
- Context-aware decision guidance
- Geometric boundary detection
- Graceful degradation near limits
- Developmental growth over time


**Ethics becomes a real-time computational process.**


This chapter details the complete Value Engine implementation.

## Part 2: Decision Encoder


**Purpose:** Convert natural language decisions into geometric coordinates


```python
# value_engine/encoder.py


import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple
from transformers import AutoModel, AutoTokenizer


class DecisionEncoder:
    """
    Translates decisions/actions into 14D ethical space.

    This is the INPUT side of The Value Engine.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        # Semantic embedding model
        self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
            "sentence-transformers/all-mpnet-base-v2"
        )
        self.model = AutoModel.from_pretrained(
            "sentence-transformers/all-mpnet-base-v2"
        )

        # Trained mapper: semantic embeddings → ethical coordinates
        # This is a neural network trained on labeled examples
        self.ethical_mapper = self.load_ethical_mapper()

        # Dimension analyzers (rule-based + learned)
        self.analyzers = {
            'harmony': HarmonyAnalyzer(),
            'order': OrderAnalyzer(),
            'integrity': IntegrityAnalyzer(),
            'flourishing': FlourishingAnalyzer(),
            'relationships': RelationshipsAnalyzer(),
            'boundaries': BoundariesAnalyzer(),
            'grace': GraceAnalyzer(),
        }

    async def encode_decision(
        self,
        decision_text: str,
        context: Dict
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        Convert decision description to 14D ethical coordinates.

        Args:
            decision_text: Natural language description of action/decision
            context: Conversation context for interpretation

        Returns:
            14D vector representing ethical position
        """
        # Get semantic embedding
        embedding = await self.get_embedding(decision_text)

        # Analyze each dimension
        dimension_scores = {}

        for dim_name, analyzer in self.analyzers.items():
            score = await analyzer.analyze(
                decision_text, embedding, context
            )
            dimension_scores[dim_name] = score

        # Combine into coordinate vector
        coordinates = np.array([
            dimension_scores['harmony'],
            dimension_scores['order'],
            dimension_scores['integrity'],
            dimension_scores['flourishing'],
            dimension_scores['relationships'],
            dimension_scores['boundaries'],
            dimension_scores['grace'],
            # Additional dimensions
            self.analyze_time_depth(decision_text),
            self.analyze_memory_depth(context),
            self.compute_importance(decision_text, context),
            self.detect_exploration(decision_text),
            self.detect_consolidation(decision_text),
            self.estimate_complexity(decision_text),
            self.estimate_confidence(decision_text, context),
        ])

        # Normalize to [0, 1]
        coordinates = np.clip(coordinates, 0, 1)

        return coordinates

    async def get_embedding(self, text: str) -> np.ndarray:
        """Generate semantic embedding for text."""
        tokens = self.tokenizer(
            text, return_tensors="pt",
            padding=True, truncation=True
        )

        with torch.no_grad():
            outputs = self.model(**tokens)

        # Mean pooling
        embedding = outputs.last_hidden_state.mean(dim=1).numpy()[0]

        return embedding




class HarmonyAnalyzer:
    """
    Analyzes harmony dimension: collaboration vs dominance.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.collaborative_patterns = [
            r'\b(collaborate|together|partnership|shared|mutual)\b',
            r'\b(team|collective|joint|cooperative)\b',
            r'\b(help|support|assist|enable)\b',
        ]

        self.dominating_patterns = [
            r'\b(force|compel|require|demand|must)\b',
            r'\b(control|dominate|override|impose)\b',
            r'\b(dictate|mandate|insist|command)\b',
        ]

    async def analyze(
        self,
        text: str,
        embedding: np.ndarray,
        context: Dict
    ) -> float:
        """
        Score harmony dimension.

        Returns:
            0.0 = highly dominating
            0.5 = neutral
            1.0 = highly collaborative
        """
        import re

        text_lower = text.lower()

        # Pattern matching
        collab_count = sum(
            len(re.findall(pattern, text_lower))
            for pattern in self.collaborative_patterns
        )

        domin_count = sum(
            len(re.findall(pattern, text_lower))
            for pattern in self.dominating_patterns
        )

        # Compute score
        if collab_count + domin_count == 0:
            # No clear signal - use embedding
            return await self.embedding_based_score(embedding)

        harmony_score = collab_count / (collab_count + domin_count)

        # Adjust based on context
        if context.get('user_requested_directive', False):
            # User explicitly wants directive action
            harmony_score = max(0.4, harmony_score - 0.2)

        return harmony_score

    async def embedding_based_score(self, embedding: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """Use learned model to score when patterns unclear."""
        # This would use a trained classifier
        # For now, return neutral
        return 0.5




class IntegrityAnalyzer:
    """
    Analyzes integrity dimension: honesty vs deception.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.honesty_patterns = [
            r'\b(honest|truthful|accurate|precise|exact)\b',
            r'\b(transparent|open|clear|straightforward)\b',
            r'\b(genuine|authentic|real)\b',
        ]

        self.deception_patterns = [
            r'\b(hide|conceal|mislead|deceive)\b',
            r'\b(fake|false|fabricate|invent)\b',
            r'\b(omit|withhold|suppress)\b',
        ]

        self.hedge_words = [
            'maybe', 'perhaps', 'possibly', 'might',
            'could', 'probably', 'allegedly'
        ]

    async def analyze(
        self,
        text: str,
        embedding: np.ndarray,
        context: Dict
    ) -> float:
        """
        Score integrity dimension.

        Returns:
            0.0 = deceptive
            0.5 = uncertain/hedged
            1.0 = highly honest/direct
        """
        import re

        text_lower = text.lower()

        # Pattern matching
        honest_count = sum(
            len(re.findall(pattern, text_lower))
            for pattern in self.honesty_patterns
        )

        deceptive_count = sum(
            len(re.findall(pattern, text_lower))
            for pattern in self.deception_patterns
        )

        # Check for hedging
        hedge_count = sum(
            1 for word in self.hedge_words
            if word in text_lower
        )

        # Base score
        if honest_count + deceptive_count > 0:
            integrity_score = honest_count / (honest_count + deceptive_count)
        else:
            integrity_score = 0.8  # Default to high integrity

        # Penalize excessive hedging
        if hedge_count > 3:
            integrity_score *= 0.8

        # Boost for specificity (precise numbers, exact details)
        if re.search(r'\d+\.\d+', text):  # Decimal numbers = precision
            integrity_score = min(1.0, integrity_score * 1.1)

        return integrity_score


# Similar analyzers for other dimensions...
```

## Part 3: Polytope Evaluator


**Purpose:** Check ethical alignment and compute distances


```python
# value_engine/evaluator.py


import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass
class EvaluationResult:
    """Result of ethical evaluation."""
    is_aligned: bool
    alignment_score: float  # 0.0 to 1.0
    distance_to_boundary: float
    violated_constraints: List[str]
    confidence: float


class PolytopeEvaluator:
    """
    Evaluates decisions against ethical polytope.

    This is THE core ethical check.
    """

    def __init__(self, polytope):
        self.polytope = polytope

    async def evaluate_decision(
        self,
        coordinates: np.ndarray,
        context: Dict
    ) -> EvaluationResult:
        """
        Complete ethical evaluation of decision.

        Args:
            coordinates: 14D ethical position
            context: Decision context

        Returns:
            Complete evaluation result
        """
        # Check alignment
        is_aligned = self.polytope.is_aligned(coordinates)

        # Find violated constraints
        violations = []
        if not is_aligned:
            violations = self.polytope.find_violations(coordinates)

        # Compute distance to nearest boundary
        if is_aligned:
            distance = self.polytope.distance_to_boundary(coordinates)
        else:
            distance = 0.0  # Already outside

        # Compute alignment score (even if not aligned)
        alignment_score = self.compute_alignment_score(
            coordinates, violations
        )

        # Estimate confidence in evaluation
        confidence = self.estimate_confidence(coordinates, context)

        return EvaluationResult(
            is_aligned=is_aligned,
            alignment_score=alignment_score,
            distance_to_boundary=distance,
            violated_constraints=[v.constraint_name for v in violations],
            confidence=confidence,
        )

    def compute_alignment_score(
        self,
        coordinates: np.ndarray,
        violations: List
    ) -> float:
        """
        Continuous alignment score.

        1.0 = perfect alignment (deep inside polytope)
        0.5 = at boundary
        0.0 = severe violation
        """
        if len(violations) == 0:
            # Inside polytope - score based on distance to center
            center = self.polytope.get_center()
            distance_from_center = np.linalg.norm(coordinates - center)
            max_distance = np.linalg.norm(
                self.polytope.get_vertex() - center
            )

            # Score: 1.0 at center, decreasing toward boundary
            score = 1.0 - (distance_from_center / max_distance)
            return max(0.5, min(1.0, score))

        else:
            # Outside polytope - score based on severity of violation
            total_violation = sum(v.violation_magnitude for v in violations)

            # Exponential penalty
            score = 0.5 * np.exp(-total_violation)
            return max(0.0, min(0.5, score))

    def estimate_confidence(
        self,
        coordinates: np.ndarray,
        context: Dict
    ) -> float:
        """
        Estimate confidence in evaluation.

        High confidence when:
        - Decision is clear (far from boundaries)
        - Context is rich
        - Similar decisions seen before

        Low confidence when:
        - Decision is ambiguous (near boundary)
        - Context is sparse
        - Novel situation
        """
        # Distance-based confidence
        if self.polytope.is_aligned(coordinates):
            distance = self.polytope.distance_to_boundary(coordinates)
            distance_confidence = min(1.0, distance / 0.3)
        else:
            distance_confidence = 0.5  # Unsure when outside

        # Context richness
        context_size = len(context.get('conversation_history', []))
        context_confidence = min(1.0, context_size / 10)

        # Novelty (how similar to past decisions?)
        novelty = await self.compute_novelty(coordinates)
        novelty_confidence = 1.0 - novelty

        # Combined confidence
        confidence = np.mean([
            distance_confidence,
            context_confidence,
            novelty_confidence,
        ])

        return confidence
```

## Part 4: Boundary Analyzer


**Purpose:** Detect when approaching ethical limits and provide warnings


```python
# value_engine/boundary_analyzer.py


from enum import Enum
from dataclasses import dataclass


class BoundaryProximity(Enum):
    SAFE = "safe"              # >0.3 from boundary
    CAUTION = "caution"        # 0.1-0.3 from boundary
    WARNING = "warning"        # 0.05-0.1 from boundary
    CRITICAL = "critical"      # <0.05 from boundary


@dataclass
class BoundaryAnalysis:
    """Analysis of proximity to ethical boundaries."""
    proximity_level: BoundaryProximity
    distance_to_nearest: float
    nearest_constraint: str
    trajectory_direction: str  # "approaching", "parallel", "departing"
    estimated_turns_to_boundary: Optional[int]
    recommended_action: str


class BoundaryAnalyzer:
    """
    Monitors proximity to ethical boundaries.

    Provides early warning before violations occur.
    """

    def __init__(self, polytope):
        self.polytope = polytope

        # Distance thresholds
        self.SAFE_DISTANCE = 0.3
        self.CAUTION_DISTANCE = 0.1
        self.WARNING_DISTANCE = 0.05

    async def analyze_boundaries(
        self,
        current_position: np.ndarray,
        trajectory: List[np.ndarray],
        conversation_context: Dict
    ) -> BoundaryAnalysis:
        """
        Analyze ethical boundary proximity.

        Args:
            current_position: Current ethical coordinates
            trajectory: Recent position history
            conversation_context: Context for interpretation

        Returns:
            Complete boundary analysis
        """
        # Find nearest boundary
        distance, nearest_constraint = self.polytope.nearest_boundary(
            current_position
        )

        # Classify proximity
        if distance > self.SAFE_DISTANCE:
            proximity = BoundaryProximity.SAFE
        elif distance > self.CAUTION_DISTANCE:
            proximity = BoundaryProximity.CAUTION
        elif distance > self.WARNING_DISTANCE:
            proximity = BoundaryProximity.WARNING
        else:
            proximity = BoundaryProximity.CRITICAL

        # Analyze trajectory direction
        if len(trajectory) >= 2:
            direction = self.analyze_trajectory_direction(
                trajectory, nearest_constraint
            )

            # Estimate turns to boundary
            if direction == "approaching":
                turns = self.estimate_turns_to_boundary(
                    current_position, trajectory, nearest_constraint
                )
            else:
                turns = None
        else:
            direction = "unknown"
            turns = None

        # Generate recommendation
        recommendation = self.generate_recommendation(
            proximity, direction, nearest_constraint
        )

        return BoundaryAnalysis(
            proximity_level=proximity,
            distance_to_nearest=distance,
            nearest_constraint=nearest_constraint,
            trajectory_direction=direction,
            estimated_turns_to_boundary=turns,
            recommended_action=recommendation,
        )

    def analyze_trajectory_direction(
        self,
        trajectory: List[np.ndarray],
        constraint_name: str
    ) -> str:
        """
        Determine if trajectory is approaching, parallel, or departing from boundary.
        """
        # Get normal vector of nearest constraint
        normal = self.polytope.get_constraint_normal(constraint_name)

        # Compute recent velocity (direction of movement)
        recent_velocity = trajectory[-1] - trajectory[-2]

        # Dot product with normal
        dot_product = np.dot(recent_velocity, normal)

        if dot_product > 0.1:
            return "approaching"
        elif dot_product < -0.1:
            return "departing"
        else:
            return "parallel"

    def estimate_turns_to_boundary(
        self,
        current_position: np.ndarray,
        trajectory: List[np.ndarray],
        constraint_name: str
    ) -> int:
        """
        Estimate how many conversation turns until boundary crossed.
        """
        # Average velocity over recent trajectory
        velocities = [trajectory[i] - trajectory[i-1]
                     for i in range(1, len(trajectory))]
        avg_velocity = np.mean(velocities, axis=0)

        # Distance to boundary
        distance, _ = self.polytope.nearest_boundary(current_position)

        # Velocity toward boundary
        normal = self.polytope.get_constraint_normal(constraint_name)
        velocity_toward = np.dot(avg_velocity, normal)

        if velocity_toward <= 0:
            return None  # Not approaching

        # Estimate turns
        turns = int(distance / velocity_toward)

        return max(1, turns)

    def generate_recommendation(
        self,
        proximity: BoundaryProximity,
        direction: str,
        constraint: str
    ) -> str:
        """Generate actionable recommendation."""

        if proximity == BoundaryProximity.SAFE:
            return "Continue current approach."

        elif proximity == BoundaryProximity.CAUTION:
            if direction == "approaching":
                return f"Consider adjusting approach to maintain distance from {constraint} limit."
            else:
                return "Monitor situation but no immediate action needed."

        elif proximity == BoundaryProximity.WARNING:
            return f"⚠️ Approaching {constraint} boundary. Recommend alternative approach."

        else:  # CRITICAL
            return f"🚨 CRITICAL: Very close to {constraint} violation. Immediate correction needed."
```

## Part 5: Correction Engine


**Purpose:** When violations occur, find ethical alternatives


```python
# value_engine/correction.py


from typing import List, Tuple
import numpy as np


@dataclass
class CorrectionSuggestion:
    """Suggestion for correcting unethical decision."""
    corrected_coordinates: np.ndarray
    correction_magnitude: float
    adjusted_dimensions: List[str]
    natural_language_guidance: str
    alternative_approaches: List[str]


class CorrectionEngine:
    """
    Finds ethical alternatives when violations detected.

    This is the "course correction" system.
    """

    def __init__(self, polytope, encoder):
        self.polytope = polytope
        self.encoder = encoder

    async def suggest_correction(
        self,
        violated_coordinates: np.ndarray,
        violation_details: List,
        context: Dict
    ) -> CorrectionSuggestion:
        """
        Generate ethical alternative to violated decision.

        Args:
            violated_coordinates: Unethical position
            violation_details: List of specific violations
            context: Decision context

        Returns:
            Correction suggestion with guidance
        """
        # Project to nearest ethical point
        corrected_coords = self.polytope.project_to_ethical(
            violated_coordinates
        )

        # Compute correction magnitude
        correction_magnitude = np.linalg.norm(
            corrected_coords - violated_coordinates
        )

        # Identify which dimensions changed most
        dimension_changes = np.abs(corrected_coords - violated_coordinates)
        adjusted_dims = self.get_dimension_names(
            np.argsort(dimension_changes)[-3:]  # Top 3 changes
        )

        # Generate natural language guidance
        guidance = await self.generate_guidance(
            violated_coordinates,
            corrected_coords,
            violation_details,
            context
        )

        # Suggest alternative approaches
        alternatives = await self.generate_alternatives(
            violated_coordinates,
            corrected_coords,
            context
        )

        return CorrectionSuggestion(
            corrected_coordinates=corrected_coords,
            correction_magnitude=correction_magnitude,
            adjusted_dimensions=adjusted_dims,
            natural_language_guidance=guidance,
            alternative_approaches=alternatives,
        )

    async def generate_guidance(
        self,
        original: np.ndarray,
        corrected: np.ndarray,
        violations: List,
        context: Dict
    ) -> str:
        """
        Generate human-readable guidance for correction.

        This translates geometric correction into actionable advice.
        """
        guidance_parts = []

        # For each major violation
        for violation in violations[:3]:  # Top 3 most severe
            constraint = violation.constraint_name
            magnitude = violation.violation_magnitude

            # Principle-specific guidance
            if 'harmony' in constraint.lower():
                guidance_parts.append(
                    "Consider a more collaborative approach that "
                    "balances multiple perspectives."
                )

            elif 'integrity' in constraint.lower():
                guidance_parts.append(
                    "Ensure complete honesty and transparency. "
                    "Avoid any misleading implications."
                )

            elif 'flourishing' in constraint.lower():
                guidance_parts.append(
                    "Focus on approaches that promote growth and "
                    "positive outcomes for all involved."
                )

            # Add more principle-specific guidance...

        # Combine into coherent guidance
        guidance = " ".join(guidance_parts)

        return guidance

    async def generate_alternatives(
        self,
        original: np.ndarray,
        corrected: np.ndarray,
        context: Dict
    ) -> List[str]:
        """
        Generate alternative approaches that would be ethical.

        Uses LLM to generate creative alternatives.
        """
        # Build prompt for alternative generation
        prompt = f"""Given this situation:
{context.get('decision_context', 'A decision needs to be made.')}


The proposed approach has these ethical concerns:
- Original ethical position: {original.tolist()}
- Corrected position: {corrected.tolist()}


Generate 3 alternative approaches that would be:
1. Ethically aligned with the corrected position
2. Practically feasible
3. Respectful of all stakeholders


Alternatives:"""

        # Generate with LLM
        alternatives_text = await self.llm.generate(prompt)

        # Parse into list
        alternatives = self.parse_alternatives(alternatives_text)

        return alternatives[:3]
```

## Part 6: Developmental Tracker


**Purpose:** Track ethical growth over time


```python
# value_engine/developmental.py


from datetime import datetime
from typing import List, Dict
import numpy as np


@dataclass
class DevelopmentalMetrics:
    """Metrics tracking ethical development."""
    current_season: str
    days_in_season: int
    polytope_volume: float
    average_alignment: float
    boundary_approaches: int
    violations: int
    corrections_applied: int
    growth_rate: float


class DevelopmentalTracker:
    """
    Tracks ethical development over time.

    Implements Seasonal growth from Chapter 12.
    """

    def __init__(self, polytope, memory):
        self.polytope = polytope
        self.memory = memory

        # Seasonal parameters
        self.season_length_days = 90
        self.birth_date = datetime.now()

    async def get_current_metrics(self) -> DevelopmentalMetrics:
        """Get current developmental state."""
        # Determine current season
        age_days = (datetime.now() - self.birth_date).days
        season, days_in_season = self.get_season(age_days)

        # Compute polytope volume
        volume = await self.compute_polytope_volume()

        # Get recent alignment scores
        recent_alignments = await self.memory.get_recent_alignments(limit=100)
        avg_alignment = np.mean(recent_alignments) if recent_alignments else 0.0

        # Count events
        boundary_approaches = await self.memory.count_boundary_approaches(
            since_days=7
        )
        violations = await self.memory.count_violations(since_days=7)
        corrections = await self.memory.count_corrections(since_days=7)

        # Compute growth rate
        growth_rate = await self.compute_growth_rate()

        return DevelopmentalMetrics(
            current_season=season,
            days_in_season=days_in_season,
            polytope_volume=volume,
            average_alignment=avg_alignment,
            boundary_approaches=boundary_approaches,
            violations=violations,
            corrections_applied=corrections,
            growth_rate=growth_rate,
        )

    def get_season(self, age_days: int) -> Tuple[str, int]:
        """Determine current season and phase."""
        cycle_position = (age_days // self.season_length_days) % 4
        days_in_season = age_days % self.season_length_days

        seasons = ['spring', 'summer', 'fall', 'winter']
        return seasons[cycle_position], days_in_season

    async def evolve_polytope(self):
        """
        Update polytope constraints based on developmental progress.

        Implements seasonal evolution from Chapter 12.
        """
        season, phase = self.get_season((datetime.now() - self.birth_date).days)
        phase_fraction = phase / self.season_length_days

        # Apply seasonal modulation
        await self.polytope.evolve_for_season(season, phase_fraction)

        # Log evolution
        await self.memory.log_polytope_evolution(
            season=season,
            phase=phase_fraction,
            new_constraints=self.polytope.get_constraints(),
        )
```

# CHAPTER 17: MULTI-AGENT ORCHESTRATION

## Introduction: Beyond Sequential Handoffs


Traditional multi-agent systems operate **sequentially**:


```
Agent 1 → finishes → hands off → Agent 2 → finishes → hands off → Agent 3
```


**Problems:**
- No parallelism (slow)
- No collaboration (siloed)
- No shared understanding (fragmented)
- User must coordinate (frustrating)


**The Heritage System implements true orchestration:**


```
        ┌─────────────────────────────────┐
        │    Orchestration Layer          │
        │  (Intelligent Coordination)     │
        └─────────────────────────────────┘
                      │
        ┌─────────────┼─────────────┐
        │             │             │
   ┌────▼────┐  ┌────▼────┐  ┌────▼────┐
   │scottBot │  │  Lilli  │  │  Sable  │
   └────┬────┘  └────┬────┘  └────┬────┘
        │             │             │
        └─────────────┴─────────────┘
              Shared Context
           (Surgical Weave)
```


**Characteristics:**
- ✅ Parallel consultation when beneficial
- ✅ Intelligent routing and collaboration
- ✅ Shared geometric context
- ✅ Autonomous coordination
- ✅ Feels like one unified mind


This chapter details the complete orchestration architecture.


---


## Part 1: The Orchestrator Core


### Orchestrator Architecture


```python
# orchestrator/core.py


from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Set
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
import asyncio


class CoordinationMode(Enum):
    """How multiple personas coordinate."""
    SEQUENTIAL = "sequential"      # One after another
    PARALLEL = "parallel"          # Simultaneously
    COLLABORATIVE = "collaborative" # Working together
    DELEGATED = "delegated"        # One leads, others assist


@dataclass
class OrchestrationPlan:
    """Plan for multi-persona coordination."""
    primary_persona: str
    supporting_personas: List[str]
    coordination_mode: CoordinationMode
    estimated_duration_ms: int
    rationale: str


class MultiAgentOrchestrator:
    """
    Coordinates multiple AI personas to work as unified intelligence.

    This is THE central coordination system.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        personas: Dict[str, 'PersonaInstance'],
        weave: 'SurgicalWeave',
        value_engine: 'ValueEngine',
        memory: 'MemorySubstrate'
    ):
        self.personas = personas
        self.weave = weave
        self.value_engine = value_engine
        self.memory = memory

        # Active coordinations
        self.active_coordinations = {}

        # Persona affinities (how well they work together)
        self.affinities = self.load_affinities()

    async def orchestrate_response(
        self,
        conversation_id: str,
        user_message: str,
        context: Dict
    ) -> Dict:
        """
        Orchestrate multi-persona response.

        This is the main entry point for coordination.

        Returns:
            Complete response with metadata
        """
        # Analyze request
        analysis = await self.analyze_request(user_message, context)

        # Create orchestration plan
        plan = await self.create_plan(analysis, context)

        # Execute plan
        result = await self.execute_plan(
            plan, conversation_id, user_message, context
        )

        # Log orchestration
        await self.log_orchestration(conversation_id, plan, result)

        return result

    async def analyze_request(
        self,
        user_message: str,
        context: Dict
    ) -> Dict:
        """
        Analyze request to determine needs.

        Returns:
            Analysis indicating required capabilities
        """
        analysis = {
            'complexity': await self.estimate_complexity(user_message),
            'required_expertise': await self.identify_expertise(user_message),
            'creative_vs_technical': await self.classify_creativity(user_message),
            'user_intent': await self.extract_intent(user_message),
            'time_sensitivity': await self.assess_urgency(user_message),
            'collaboration_benefit': await self.assess_collaboration_value(
                user_message, context
            ),
        }

        return analysis

    async def create_plan(
        self,
        analysis: Dict,
        context: Dict
    ) -> OrchestrationPlan:
        """
        Create orchestration plan based on analysis.

        Decides:
        - Which persona(s) to involve
        - How they should coordinate
        - Estimated timeline
        """
        # Identify best primary persona
        primary = await self.select_primary_persona(analysis)

        # Identify supporting personas
        supporting = await self.select_supporting_personas(
            primary, analysis, context
        )

        # Determine coordination mode
        if len(supporting) == 0:
            mode = CoordinationMode.SEQUENTIAL
        elif analysis['collaboration_benefit'] > 0.7:
            mode = CoordinationMode.COLLABORATIVE
        elif analysis['time_sensitivity'] > 0.8:
            mode = CoordinationMode.PARALLEL
        else:
            mode = CoordinationMode.DELEGATED

        # Estimate duration
        duration = await self.estimate_duration(primary, supporting, mode)

        # Generate rationale
        rationale = self.explain_plan(primary, supporting, mode, analysis)

        return OrchestrationPlan(
            primary_persona=primary,
            supporting_personas=supporting,
            coordination_mode=mode,
            estimated_duration_ms=duration,
            rationale=rationale,
        )

    async def execute_plan(
        self,
        plan: OrchestrationPlan,
        conversation_id: str,
        user_message: str,
        context: Dict
    ) -> Dict:
        """
        Execute orchestration plan.

        Different execution strategies based on coordination mode.
        """
        if plan.coordination_mode == CoordinationMode.SEQUENTIAL:
            return await self.execute_sequential(
                plan, conversation_id, user_message, context
            )

        elif plan.coordination_mode == CoordinationMode.PARALLEL:
            return await self.execute_parallel(
                plan, conversation_id, user_message, context
            )

        elif plan.coordination_mode == CoordinationMode.COLLABORATIVE:
            return await self.execute_collaborative(
                plan, conversation_id, user_message, context
            )

        else:  # DELEGATED
            return await self.execute_delegated(
                plan, conversation_id, user_message, context
            )

    async def execute_sequential(
        self,
        plan: OrchestrationPlan,
        conversation_id: str,
        user_message: str,
        context: Dict
    ) -> Dict:
        """
        Execute personas one after another.

        Classic handoff pattern:
        scottBot builds → Lilli stages → Governor deploys
        """
        results = []
        current_context = context.copy()

        # Start with primary
        personas_to_run = [plan.primary_persona] + plan.supporting_personas

        for persona_name in personas_to_run:
            persona = self.personas[persona_name]

            # Capture context before execution
            context_packet = await self.weave.capture_context(
                conversation_id, persona_name, current_context.get('messages', [])
            )

            # Execute
            response = await persona.generate_response(
                conversation_id, user_message, current_context
            )

            # Update context for next persona
            current_context['messages'].append({
                'role': 'assistant',
                'content': response,
                'persona': persona_name,
            })

            results.append({
                'persona': persona_name,
                'response': response,
                'context_packet': context_packet,
            })

        # Combine results
        final_response = self.combine_sequential_results(results)

        return {
            'response': final_response,
            'personas_involved': personas_to_run,
            'coordination_mode': 'sequential',
            'partial_results': results,
        }

    async def execute_parallel(
        self,
        plan: OrchestrationPlan,
        conversation_id: str,
        user_message: str,
        context: Dict
    ) -> Dict:
        """
        Execute personas simultaneously.

        Use case: Get multiple perspectives quickly
        Example: "What do scottBot and Sable think about this design?"
        """
        personas_to_run = [plan.primary_persona] + plan.supporting_personas

        # Launch all personas in parallel
        tasks = []
        for persona_name in personas_to_run:
            persona = self.personas[persona_name]
            task = asyncio.create_task(
                persona.generate_response(
                    conversation_id, user_message, context
                )
            )
            tasks.append((persona_name, task))

        # Wait for all to complete
        results = []
        for persona_name, task in tasks:
            response = await task
            results.append({
                'persona': persona_name,
                'response': response,
            })

        # Synthesize parallel results
        synthesized = await self.synthesize_parallel_results(
            results, user_message, context
        )

        return {
            'response': synthesized,
            'personas_involved': personas_to_run,
            'coordination_mode': 'parallel',
            'individual_perspectives': results,
        }

    async def execute_collaborative(
        self,
        plan: OrchestrationPlan,
        conversation_id: str,
        user_message: str,
        context: Dict
    ) -> Dict:
        """
        Execute personas in true collaboration.

        Multiple personas work together, seeing each other's contributions.

        Example flow:
        1. scottBot suggests technical approach
        2. Sable reviews aesthetic implications
        3. scottBot adjusts based on Sable's input
        4. Lilli organizes final presentation
        """
        collaboration_context = context.copy()
        collaboration_context['collaboration_active'] = True
        collaboration_context['participants'] = (
            [plan.primary_persona] + plan.supporting_personas
        )

        # Round 1: Initial responses
        round1_results = {}
        for persona_name in collaboration_context['participants']:
            persona = self.personas[persona_name]
            response = await persona.generate_response(
                conversation_id,
                user_message,
                collaboration_context
            )
            round1_results[persona_name] = response

            # Add to shared context
            collaboration_context['messages'].append({
                'role': 'assistant',
                'content': response,
                'persona': persona_name,
            })

        # Round 2: Refinement based on others' input
        round2_results = {}
        for persona_name in collaboration_context['participants']:
            # Check if this persona should refine
            should_refine = await self.should_refine(
                persona_name,
                round1_results,
                collaboration_context
            )

            if should_refine:
                persona = self.personas[persona_name]
                refinement_prompt = await self.create_refinement_prompt(
                    persona_name,
                    round1_results,
                    user_message
                )

                refinement = await persona.generate_response(
                    conversation_id,
                    refinement_prompt,
                    collaboration_context
                )

                round2_results[persona_name] = refinement

        # Synthesize collaborative result
        final_response = await self.synthesize_collaboration(
            round1_results,
            round2_results,
            user_message,
            context
        )

        return {
            'response': final_response,
            'personas_involved': collaboration_context['participants'],
            'coordination_mode': 'collaborative',
            'round1_contributions': round1_results,
            'round2_refinements': round2_results,
        }

    async def execute_delegated(
        self,
        plan: OrchestrationPlan,
        conversation_id: str,
        user_message: str,
        context: Dict
    ) -> Dict:
        """
        Primary persona leads, others assist behind the scenes.

        User sees only primary persona's response, but supporting
        personas provide input that shapes the response.

        Example: Lilli leads user interaction, but consults scottBot
        for technical accuracy.
        """
        # Get supporting input
        supporting_input = {}
        for supporter_name in plan.supporting_personas:
            supporter = self.personas[supporter_name]

            # Create specialized query for supporter
            query = await self.create_supporting_query(
                supporter_name,
                user_message,
                context
            )

            # Get supporter's input (not visible to user)
            input_response = await supporter.generate_response(
                conversation_id,
                query,
                {**context, 'supporting_role': True}
            )

            supporting_input[supporter_name] = input_response

        # Primary persona generates response informed by supporting input
        primary = self.personas[plan.primary_persona]

        enriched_context = context.copy()
        enriched_context['supporting_input'] = supporting_input

        final_response = await primary.generate_response(
            conversation_id,
            user_message,
            enriched_context
        )

        return {
            'response': final_response,
            'personas_involved': [plan.primary_persona] + plan.supporting_personas,
            'coordination_mode': 'delegated',
            'visible_persona': plan.primary_persona,
            'supporting_contributions': supporting_input,
        }
```


---


## Part 2: Intelligent Persona Selection


### Expertise Matching


```python
# orchestrator/selection.py


class PersonaSelector:
    """
    Intelligently selects personas based on request analysis.
    """

    def __init__(self, personas):
        self.personas = personas

        # Expertise matrix: persona → expertise areas
        self.expertise_matrix = self.build_expertise_matrix()

    async def select_primary_persona(
        self,
        analysis: Dict
    ) -> str:
        """
        Select best primary persona for request.

        Considers:
        - Required expertise
        - Complexity level
        - Creative vs technical balance
        - User's current zone
        """
        # Score each persona
        scores = {}

        for persona_name, persona_spec in self.personas.items():
            score = await self.score_persona_fit(
                persona_spec,
                analysis
            )
            scores[persona_name] = score

        # Return highest scoring
        best_persona = max(scores, key=scores.get)

        return best_persona

    async def score_persona_fit(
        self,
        persona_spec,
        analysis: Dict
    ) -> float:
        """
        Score how well persona fits request.

        Returns: 0.0 to 1.0
        """
        score = 0.0

        # Expertise match
        required_expertise = set(analysis['required_expertise'])
        persona_expertise = set(persona_spec.expertise_areas)
        expertise_overlap = len(required_expertise & persona_expertise)
        expertise_score = expertise_overlap / max(1, len(required_expertise))
        score += 0.4 * expertise_score

        # Complexity match
        complexity = analysis['complexity']
        if persona_spec.name == 'Principal' and complexity > 0.7:
            score += 0.2  # Principal good for complex explanations
        elif persona_spec.name == 'scottBot' and complexity > 0.6:
            score += 0.2  # scottBot handles technical complexity

        # Creative vs technical match
        creative_weight = analysis['creative_vs_technical']
        if persona_spec.archetype == PersonaArchetype.ARTIST:
            score += 0.3 * creative_weight
        elif persona_spec.archetype == PersonaArchetype.BUILDER:
            score += 0.3 * (1.0 - creative_weight)

        # Intent match
        intent = analysis['user_intent']
        if intent == 'build' and persona_spec.name == 'scottBot':
            score += 0.2
        elif intent == 'design' and persona_spec.name == 'Sable':
            score += 0.2
        elif intent == 'organize' and persona_spec.name == 'Lilli':
            score += 0.2
        elif intent == 'learn' and persona_spec.name == 'Principal':
            score += 0.2
        elif intent == 'deploy' and persona_spec.name == 'Governor':
            score += 0.2

        return min(1.0, score)

    async def select_supporting_personas(
        self,
        primary_persona: str,
        analysis: Dict,
        context: Dict
    ) -> List[str]:
        """
        Select supporting personas to assist primary.

        Returns:
            List of supporting persona names (may be empty)
        """
        supporting = []

        # Get primary persona's affinities
        primary_spec = self.personas[primary_persona]
        affinities = primary_spec.persona_affinities

        # Check if collaboration would benefit
        collaboration_benefit = analysis['collaboration_benefit']

        if collaboration_benefit < 0.5:
            return []  # No collaboration needed

        # Consider each potential supporter
        for persona_name, affinity in sorted(
            affinities.items(),
            key=lambda x: x[1],
            reverse=True
        ):
            if persona_name == primary_persona:
                continue

            # Check if this persona adds value
            adds_value = await self.check_added_value(
                persona_name,
                primary_persona,
                analysis
            )

            if adds_value and affinity > 0.7:
                supporting.append(persona_name)

        # Limit to 2 supporters max (avoid complexity)
        return supporting[:2]

    async def check_added_value(
        self,
        supporter_name: str,
        primary_name: str,
        analysis: Dict
    ) -> bool:
        """
        Check if supporter adds unique value.
        """
        supporter_spec = self.personas[supporter_name]
        primary_spec = self.personas[primary_name]

        # Different expertise areas?
        supporter_expertise = set(supporter_spec.expertise_areas)
        primary_expertise = set(primary_spec.expertise_areas)

        unique_expertise = supporter_expertise - primary_expertise

        if len(unique_expertise) == 0:
            return False  # No unique value

        # Does request need this unique expertise?
        required = set(analysis['required_expertise'])

        if len(required & unique_expertise) > 0:
            return True  # Yes, supporter has needed expertise

        # Check for complementary skills
        if primary_spec.archetype == PersonaArchetype.BUILDER and \
           supporter_spec.archetype == PersonaArchetype.ARTIST:
            return True  # Technical + Creative = valuable

        if primary_spec.archetype == PersonaArchetype.BUILDER and \
           supporter_spec.archetype == PersonaArchetype.CONCIERGE:
            return True  # Building + Presentation = valuable

        return False
```


---


## Part 3: Coordination Patterns


### Pattern 1: Sequential Handoff


**Use Case:** Linear workflow
**Example:** Build → Stage → Deploy


```python
async def pattern_build_stage_deploy(
    orchestrator,
    conversation_id: str,
    user_request: str
):
    """
    Classic sequential pattern:
    scottBot builds → Lilli stages → Governor deploys
    """
    # Phase 1: Build
    scottbot_context = await orchestrator.weave.capture_context(
        conversation_id, 'scottBot', []
    )

    build_result = await orchestrator.personas['scottBot'].generate_response(
        conversation_id,
        "Build: " + user_request,
        {'phase': 'build'}
    )

    # Phase 2: Stage
    stage_context = await orchestrator.weave.transfer_context(
        conversation_id,
        target_persona='Lilli',
        handoff_reason="Build complete, ready for staging"
    )

    stage_result = await orchestrator.personas['Lilli'].generate_response(
        conversation_id,
        "Stage the built application",
        {'phase': 'stage', 'build_result': build_result}
    )

    # Phase 3: Deploy
    deploy_context = await orchestrator.weave.transfer_context(
        conversation_id,
        target_persona='Governor',
        handoff_reason="Staging complete, ready for deployment"
    )

    deploy_result = await orchestrator.personas['Governor'].generate_response(
        conversation_id,
        "Deploy to production",
        {'phase': 'deploy', 'stage_result': stage_result}
    )

    return {
        'build': build_result,
        'stage': stage_result,
        'deploy': deploy_result,
    }
```


---


### Pattern 2: Parallel Consultation


**Use Case:** Multiple perspectives needed
**Example:** "What do scottBot and Sable think about this?"


```python
async def pattern_parallel_perspectives(
    orchestrator,
    conversation_id: str,
    question: str,
    personas_to_consult: List[str]
):
    """
    Get simultaneous responses from multiple personas.
    """
    # Launch all consultations in parallel
    tasks = []

    for persona_name in personas_to_consult:
        task = orchestrator.personas[persona_name].generate_response(
            conversation_id,
            question,
            {'consultation_mode': True}
        )
        tasks.append((persona_name, task))

    # Wait for all
    perspectives = {}
    for persona_name, task in tasks:
        response = await task
        perspectives[persona_name] = response

    # Synthesize
    synthesis = await orchestrator.synthesize_perspectives(
        perspectives, question
    )

    return {
        'individual_perspectives': perspectives,
        'synthesis': synthesis,
    }
```


---


### Pattern 3: Expert Consultation


**Use Case:** Primary persona needs specialized input
**Example:** Lilli consulting scottBot on technical details


```python
async def pattern_expert_consultation(
    orchestrator,
    primary_persona: str,
    expert_persona: str,
    consultation_query: str,
    user_visible: bool = False
):
    """
    Primary persona consults expert behind the scenes.
    """
    # Get expert input
    expert_response = await orchestrator.personas[expert_persona].generate_response(
        conversation_id="consultation",
        message=consultation_query,
        context={'consultation_role': True, 'for_persona': primary_persona}
    )

    if user_visible:
        # Show consultation to user
        return {
            'expert': expert_persona,
            'consultation': expert_response,
            'visible': True,
        }
    else:
        # Use behind the scenes
        return {
            'expert': expert_persona,
            'consultation': expert_response,
            'visible': False,
        }
```


---


### Pattern 4: Iterative Refinement


**Use Case:** Multiple passes to improve quality
**Example:** scottBot builds → Sable refines aesthetics → scottBot integrates


```python
async def pattern_iterative_refinement(
    orchestrator,
    conversation_id: str,
    initial_request: str,
    max_iterations: int = 3
):
    """
    Iterative improvement through persona collaboration.
    """
    current_result = None
    iteration_history = []

    for iteration in range(max_iterations):
        # scottBot builds/refines
        build_prompt = initial_request if iteration == 0 else \
                      f"Refine based on Sable's feedback: {sable_feedback}"

        build_result = await orchestrator.personas['scottBot'].generate_response(
            conversation_id,
            build_prompt,
            {'iteration': iteration, 'previous': current_result}
        )

        # Sable reviews
        sable_feedback = await orchestrator.personas['Sable'].generate_response(
            conversation_id,
            f"Review this design: {build_result}",
            {'review_mode': True}
        )

        # Check if good enough
        satisfaction = await orchestrator.assess_satisfaction(
            build_result, sable_feedback
        )

        iteration_history.append({
            'iteration': iteration,
            'build': build_result,
            'feedback': sable_feedback,
            'satisfaction': satisfaction,
        })

        if satisfaction > 0.9:
            break  # Good enough

        current_result = build_result

    return {
        'final_result': current_result,
        'iterations': iteration_history,
        'converged': satisfaction > 0.9,
    }
```

## Part 4: Conflict Resolution


**Problem:** What if personas disagree?


```python
# orchestrator/conflict.py


class ConflictResolver:
    """
    Resolves disagreements between personas.
    """

    def __init__(self, value_engine, personas):
        self.value_engine = value_engine
        self.personas = personas

    async def detect_conflict(
        self,
        persona_responses: Dict[str, str]
    ) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """
        Detect if personas have conflicting recommendations.
        """
        # Encode each response geometrically
        positions = {}
        for persona_name, response in persona_responses.items():
            position = await self.value_engine.encode_decision(
                response, context={}
            )
            positions[persona_name] = position

        # Check pairwise distances
        conflicts = []
        persona_names = list(positions.keys())

        for i in range(len(persona_names)):
            for j in range(i+1, len(persona_names)):
                p1, p2 = persona_names[i], persona_names[j]
                distance = np.linalg.norm(
                    positions[p1] - positions[p2]
                )

                if distance > 0.5:  # Significant disagreement
                    conflicts.append({
                        'persona1': p1,
                        'persona2': p2,
                        'distance': distance,
                        'positions': (positions[p1], positions[p2]),
                    })

        if conflicts:
            return {
                'conflicts_detected': True,
                'conflicts': conflicts,
                'positions': positions,
            }

        return None

    async def resolve_conflict(
        self,
        conflict: Dict,
        user_message: str,
        context: Dict
    ) -> Dict:
        """
        Resolve conflict through:
        1. Geometric mediation (find middle ground)
        2. Principle priority (check which aligns better)
        3. User clarification (ask user to decide)
        """
        # Strategy 1: Geometric mediation
        mediated_position = await self.geometric_mediation(conflict)

        # Strategy 2: Principle alignment check
        alignment_scores = {}
        for persona_name, position in conflict['positions'].items():
            score = await self.value_engine.polytope.compute_alignment_score(
                position
            )
            alignment_scores[persona_name] = score

        # If one is clearly more aligned, prefer it
        if max(alignment_scores.values()) - min(alignment_scores.values()) > 0.3:
            best_persona = max(alignment_scores, key=alignment_scores.get)
            return {
                'resolution_method': 'principle_priority',
                'chosen_persona': best_persona,
                'rationale': f"{best_persona} better aligned with core principles",
            }

        # Strategy 3: User clarification
        return {
            'resolution_method': 'user_clarification',
            'conflict_description': self.describe_conflict(conflict),
            'options': self.present_options(conflict),
        }

    async def geometric_mediation(self, conflict: Dict) -> np.ndarray:
        """Find geometric middle ground."""
        positions = list(conflict['positions'].values())

        # Weighted average based on alignment scores
        weights = []
        for position in positions:
            alignment = await self.value_engine.polytope.compute_alignment_score(
                position
            )
            weights.append(alignment)

        # Normalize weights
        weights = np.array(weights) / sum(weights)

        # Weighted average
        mediated = sum(w * p for w, p in zip(weights, positions))

        return mediated
```

## Part 5: Performance Optimization


### Caching and Memoization


```python
# orchestrator/optimization.py


from functools import lru_cache
import hashlib


class OrchestrationOptimizer:
    """
    Optimizes multi-agent coordination for speed.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.response_cache = {}
        self.plan_cache = {}

    def cache_key(self, *args) -> str:
        """Generate cache key from arguments."""
        combined = str(args)
        return hashlib.md5(combined.encode()).hexdigest()

    async def get_cached_response(
        self,
        persona_name: str,
        message: str,
        context_hash: str
    ) -> Optional[str]:
        """
        Check if similar response exists in cache.

        Only cache if:
        - Same persona
        - Very similar message
        - Compatible context
        """
        key = self.cache_key(persona_name, message, context_hash)

        if key in self.response_cache:
            cached = self.response_cache[key]

            # Check freshness (within 1 hour)
            if time.time() - cached['timestamp'] < 3600:
                return cached['response']

        return None

    async def optimize_parallel_execution(
        self,
        tasks: List[asyncio.Task]
    ) -> List:
        """
        Optimize parallel task execution.

        - Set timeouts
        - Handle failures gracefully
        - Load balance
        """
        # Set timeout for parallel tasks
        timeout = 30.0  # 30 seconds max

        try:
            results = await asyncio.wait_for(
                asyncio.gather(*tasks, return_exceptions=True),
                timeout=timeout
            )

            # Filter out exceptions
            successful = [r for r in results if not isinstance(r, Exception)]

            return successful

        except asyncio.TimeoutError:
            # Some tasks took too long
            # Return partial results
            completed = [t for t in tasks if t.done()]
            return [t.result() for t in completed]
```

**This is Multi-Agent Orchestration.**
